      SUBROUTINE ML5_0_MP_COEF_CONSTRUCTION_1(P,NHEL,H,IC)
C     
      USE ML5_0_POLYNOMIAL_CONSTANTS
      USE ALOHA_OBJECT
      USE, INTRINSIC :: IEEE_EXCEPTIONS
      IMPLICIT NONE
C     
C     CONSTANTS
C     
      INTEGER    NEXTERNAL
      PARAMETER (NEXTERNAL=6)
      INTEGER    NCOMB
      PARAMETER (NCOMB=64)

      INTEGER NBORNAMPS
      PARAMETER (NBORNAMPS=40)
      INTEGER    NLOOPS, NLOOPGROUPS, NCTAMPS
      PARAMETER (NLOOPS=5066, NLOOPGROUPS=1218, NCTAMPS=2022)
      INTEGER    NLOOPAMPS
      PARAMETER (NLOOPAMPS=7088)
      INTEGER    NWAVEFUNCS,NLOOPWAVEFUNCS
      PARAMETER (NWAVEFUNCS=155,NLOOPWAVEFUNCS=6898)
      REAL*16     ZERO
      PARAMETER (ZERO=0.0E0_16)
      COMPLEX*32     IZERO
      PARAMETER (IZERO=CMPLX(0.0E0_16,0.0E0_16,KIND=16))
C     These are constants related to the split orders
      INTEGER    NSO, NSQUAREDSO, NAMPSO
      PARAMETER (NSO=1, NSQUAREDSO=1, NAMPSO=2)
C     
C     ARGUMENTS
C     
      REAL*16 P(0:3,NEXTERNAL)
      INTEGER NHEL(NEXTERNAL), IC(NEXTERNAL)
      INTEGER H
C     
C     LOCAL VARIABLES
C     
      LOGICAL WL_ENTRY_INEXACT
      INTEGER I,J,K
      INTEGER FLAVOR(NEXTERNAL)
      DATA FLAVOR /NEXTERNAL*1/
      COMPLEX*32 COEFS(MAXLWFSIZE,0:VERTEXMAXCOEFS-1,MAXLWFSIZE)
C     
C     GLOBAL VARIABLES
C     

      INCLUDE 'mp_coupl_same_name.inc'

      INTEGER GOODHEL(NCOMB)
      LOGICAL GOODAMP(NSQUAREDSO,NLOOPGROUPS)
      COMMON/ML5_0_FILTERS/GOODAMP,GOODHEL

      INTEGER SQSO_TARGET
      COMMON/ML5_0_SOCHOICE/SQSO_TARGET

      LOGICAL UVCT_REQ_SO_DONE,MP_UVCT_REQ_SO_DONE,CT_REQ_SO_DONE
     $ ,MP_CT_REQ_SO_DONE,LOOP_REQ_SO_DONE,MP_LOOP_REQ_SO_DONE
     $ ,CTCALL_REQ_SO_DONE,FILTER_SO
      COMMON/ML5_0_SO_REQS/UVCT_REQ_SO_DONE,MP_UVCT_REQ_SO_DONE
     $ ,CT_REQ_SO_DONE,MP_CT_REQ_SO_DONE,LOOP_REQ_SO_DONE
     $ ,MP_LOOP_REQ_SO_DONE,CTCALL_REQ_SO_DONE,FILTER_SO

      COMPLEX*32 AMP(NBORNAMPS)
      COMMON/ML5_0_MP_AMPS/AMP
      TYPE(MP_ALOHA) W(NWAVEFUNCS)
      COMMON/ML5_0_MP_W/W

      COMPLEX*32 WL(MAXLWFSIZE,0:LOOPMAXCOEFS-1,MAXLWFSIZE,
     $ -1:NLOOPWAVEFUNCS)
      COMPLEX*32 PL(0:3,-1:NLOOPWAVEFUNCS)
      COMMON/ML5_0_MP_WL/WL,PL

      COMPLEX*32 AMPL(3,NCTAMPS)
      COMMON/ML5_0_MP_AMPL/AMPL

C     
C     ----------
C     BEGIN CODE
C     ----------

C     The target squared split order contribution is already reached
C      if true.
      IF (FILTER_SO.AND.MP_LOOP_REQ_SO_DONE) THEN
        GOTO 1001
      ENDIF

C     Coefficient construction for loop diagram with ID 41
      CALL MP_FFV1L1P0_3(PL(0,0),W(9),GC_3,ZERO,ZERO,PL(0,1),COEFS)
      CALL MP_ML5_0_UPDATE_WL_0_0(WL(1,0,1,0),4,COEFS,4,4,WL(1,0,1,1))
      CALL MP_FFV1L3_2(PL(0,1),W(35),GC_3,ZERO,ZERO,PL(0,2),COEFS)
      CALL IEEE_GET_FLAG(IEEE_INEXACT,WL_ENTRY_INEXACT)
      CALL MP_ML5_0_TERMINAL_WL_0_1(WL(1,0,1,1),4,COEFS,4,4,WL(1,0,1,2)
     $ ,WL_ENTRY_INEXACT)
      CALL MP_ML5_0_CREATE_LOOP_COEFS(WL(1,0,1,2),1,4,1,1,1,2023,H)
C     Coefficient construction for loop diagram with ID 42
      CALL MP_FFV1L2P0_3(PL(0,0),W(8),GC_3,ZERO,ZERO,PL(0,3),COEFS)
      CALL MP_ML5_0_UPDATE_WL_0_0(WL(1,0,1,0),4,COEFS,4,4,WL(1,0,1,3))
      CALL MP_FFV1L3_1(PL(0,3),W(36),GC_3,ZERO,ZERO,PL(0,4),COEFS)
      CALL IEEE_GET_FLAG(IEEE_INEXACT,WL_ENTRY_INEXACT)
      CALL MP_ML5_0_TERMINAL_WL_0_1(WL(1,0,1,3),4,COEFS,4,4,WL(1,0,1,4)
     $ ,WL_ENTRY_INEXACT)
      CALL MP_ML5_0_CREATE_LOOP_COEFS(WL(1,0,1,4),1,4,2,1,1,2024,H)
C     Coefficient construction for loop diagram with ID 43
      CALL MP_FFV1L2_1(PL(0,0),W(6),GC_3,ZERO,ZERO,PL(0,5),COEFS)
      CALL MP_ML5_0_UPDATE_WL_0_1(WL(1,0,1,0),4,COEFS,4,4,WL(1,0,1,5))
      CALL MP_FFV1L2P0_3(PL(0,5),W(8),GC_3,ZERO,ZERO,PL(0,6),COEFS)
      CALL MP_ML5_0_UPDATE_WL_1_0(WL(1,0,1,5),4,COEFS,4,4,WL(1,0,1,6))
      CALL MP_FFV1L3_1(PL(0,6),W(9),GC_3,ZERO,ZERO,PL(0,7),COEFS)
      CALL IEEE_GET_FLAG(IEEE_INEXACT,WL_ENTRY_INEXACT)
      CALL MP_ML5_0_TERMINAL_WL_1_1(WL(1,0,1,6),4,COEFS,4,4,WL(1,0,1,7)
     $ ,WL_ENTRY_INEXACT)
      CALL MP_ML5_0_CREATE_LOOP_COEFS(WL(1,0,1,7),2,4,3,1,1,2025,H)
C     Coefficient construction for loop diagram with ID 44

     $ ,10),WL_ENTRY_INEXACT)
